    function stateParentId(state) {
      return state?.parentId || null;
    }

    function statesInLayer(layerId = currentLayerId) {
      const id = layerId || null;
      return model.states.filter(state => stateParentId(state) === id);
    }

    function rootLayerContainer() {
      return {
        id: ROOT_LAYER_ID,
        title: "Wurzel",
        parentId: null,
        boundary: normalizeBoundaryConfig(model.boundary),
        rootLayer: true
      };
    }

    function currentLayerContainer() {
      return currentLayerId ? byId(currentLayerId) : rootLayerContainer();
    }

    function layerChildParentId(layer) {
      return layer?.rootLayer ? null : layer?.id || null;
    }

    let _visibleFrameSnapshot = null;

    function computeVisibleStates(layerId = currentLayerId) {
      recordPerfMetric("visibleGraphBuilds");
      const parent = layerId ? byId(layerId) : rootLayerContainer();
      const children = statesInLayer(layerId);
      const normalizedLayerId = layerId || null;
      const visibleChildren = children;
      syncBoundaryProxies(parent, visibleChildren);
      const proxies = [..._virtualNodes.values()].filter(state => state.virtualProxy?.layerId === normalizedLayerId);
      return [...visibleChildren, ...proxies];
    }

    function visibleStates(layerId = currentLayerId) {
      const normalizedLayerId = layerId || null;
      if (_visibleFrameSnapshot?.layerId === normalizedLayerId) return _visibleFrameSnapshot.states;
      return computeVisibleStates(layerId);
    }

    function transitionInLayer(transition, layerId = currentLayerId) {
      const from = byId(transition?.from);
      const to = byId(transition?.to);
      return Boolean(from && to && stateParentId(from) === stateParentId(to) && stateParentId(from) === (layerId || null));
    }

    function transitionsInLayer(layerId = currentLayerId) {
      return model.transitions.filter(transition => transitionInLayer(transition, layerId));
    }

    function computeVisibleTransitions(layerId = currentLayerId) {
      return transitionsInLayer(layerId);
    }

    function visibleTransitions(layerId = currentLayerId) {
      const normalizedLayerId = layerId || null;
      if (_visibleFrameSnapshot?.layerId === normalizedLayerId && _visibleFrameSnapshot.transitions) {
        return _visibleFrameSnapshot.transitions;
      }
      return computeVisibleTransitions(layerId);
    }

    function edgeTipHandlePointForRoute(routePoints) {

    function shouldIgnoreMapWheel(evt) {
      const target = evt.target;
      if (target?.closest?.(".state-explorer, .inspector, input, textarea, select, button")) return true;
      const horizontalIntent = Math.abs(evt.deltaX) > Math.abs(evt.deltaY);
      return horizontalIntent && !(evt.ctrlKey || evt.metaKey);
    }

    function hasActiveCanvasMotion() {
      return Boolean(boxSelecting || dragging || connecting || panning);
    }

    function cancelQueuedCanvasMove() {
      if (canvasMoveFrame) cancelAnimationFrame(canvasMoveFrame);
      canvasMoveFrame = 0;
      queuedCanvasMoveEvent = null;
    }

    function flushQueuedCanvasMove(fallbackEvent = null) {
      const evt = queuedCanvasMoveEvent || fallbackEvent;
      cancelQueuedCanvasMove();
      if (evt && hasActiveCanvasMotion()) handleCanvasMove(evt);
    }

    function queueCanvasMove(evt) {
      if (!hasActiveCanvasMotion()) return;
      queuedCanvasMoveEvent = evt;
      if (canvasMoveFrame) return;
      canvasMoveFrame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        const queuedEvent = queuedCanvasMoveEvent;
        canvasMoveFrame = 0;
        queuedCanvasMoveEvent = null;
        if (queuedEvent && hasActiveCanvasMotion()) handleCanvasMove(queuedEvent);
      });
    }

    function denseGridObstacleRoutePoints(from, to, transition = null) {
      if (obstacleSearchEndpointIsBlocked(from, to, transition)) return null;
      recordRouteMetric("obstacleSearches");
      const startPort = snapPointToGrid(edgePortPoint(from, "out", transition));
      const goalPort = snapPointToGrid(edgePortPoint(to, "in", transition));
      const start = edgeApproachPoint(from, "out", transition);
      const goal = edgeApproachPoint(to, "in", transition);
      const routeTransition = transition || { from: from.id, to: to.id };
      const bounds = routeSearchBounds(start, goal, routeTransition);

      const minBoundsGx = Math.round(bounds.minX / GRID_SIZE);
      const maxBoundsGx = Math.round(bounds.maxX / GRID_SIZE);
      const minBoundsGy = Math.round(bounds.minY / GRID_SIZE);
      const maxBoundsGy = Math.round(bounds.maxY / GRID_SIZE);

      const startGx = Math.round(start.x / GRID_SIZE);
      const startGy = Math.round(start.y / GRID_SIZE);
      const goalGx = Math.round(goal.x / GRID_SIZE);
      const goalGy = Math.round(goal.y / GRID_SIZE);

      // Precompute blocked cells (offset by 1 grid cell for clearance, except for from/to states)
      const blockedCells = new Set();
      const states = visibleStates();
      for (const state of states) {
        if (state.id === from.id || state.id === to.id) continue;
        const minGx = Math.floor((state.x - GRID_SIZE) / GRID_SIZE);
        const maxGx = Math.ceil((state.x + nodeWidth(state) + GRID_SIZE) / GRID_SIZE);
        const minGy = Math.floor((state.y - GRID_SIZE) / GRID_SIZE);
        const maxGy = Math.ceil((state.y + nodeHeight(state) + GRID_SIZE) / GRID_SIZE);
        for (let gx = minGx; gx < maxGx; gx++) {
          for (let gy = minGy; gy < maxGy; gy++) {
            blockedCells.add((gx << 16) | (gy & 0xffff));
          }

    function routeSegmentRecords(routes) {

    function overlappingRouteGroups(routes) {
      const buckets = new Map();
      for (const segment of routeSegmentRecords(routes)) {
        if (!segment.laneable) continue;
        const key = `${segment.orientation}:${segment.coordinate}`;
        if (!buckets.has(key)) buckets.set(key, []);
        buckets.get(key).push(segment);
      }

      const groups = [];
      for (const bucket of buckets.values()) {
        const sorted = bucket
          .slice()
          .sort((a, b) => a.min - b.min || a.max - b.max || edgeOrder(a.transitionId) - edgeOrder(b.transitionId));
        let group = [];
        let groupMax = -Infinity;
        for (const segment of sorted) {
          if (!group.length || segment.min < groupMax) {
            group.push(segment);
            groupMax = Math.max(groupMax, segment.max);
          } else {
            if (new Set(group.map(item => item.transitionId)).size > 1) groups.push(group);
            group = [segment];
            groupMax = segment.max;
          }
        }
        if (new Set(group.map(item => item.transitionId)).size > 1) groups.push(group);
      }
      return groups;
    }

    function createSvgSync(container, fragment, metrics = {}) {
      const old = new Map();
      container.querySelectorAll("[data-view-key]").forEach(el => {
        const key = el.getAttribute("data-view-key");
        const previous = old.get(key);
        if (previous) previous.remove();
        old.set(key, el);
      });
      return {
        container,
        fragment,
        old,
        next: new Map(),
        createMetric: metrics.createMetric || "svgElementsCreated",
        reuseMetric: metrics.reuseMetric || "svgElementsReused",
        removeMetric: metrics.removeMetric || "svgElementsRemoved"
      };
    }

    function syncSvgElement(sync, key, name, attrs = {}) {
      const normalizedName = String(name).toLowerCase();
      let el = sync.old.get(key);
      if (el) sync.old.delete(key);
      if (el && el.tagName.toLowerCase() === normalizedName) {
        recordPerfMetric(sync.reuseMetric);
      } else {
        if (el) el.remove();
        el = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", name);
        recordPerfMetric(sync.createMetric);
      }
      setSvgAttrs(el, attrs);
      el.setAttribute("data-view-key", key);
      sync.next.set(key, el);
      sync.fragment.appendChild(el);
      return el;
    }

    function finishSvgSync(sync) {
      sync.old.forEach(el => {
        el.remove();
        recordPerfMetric(sync.removeMetric);
      });
      if (sync.fragment.childNodes.length) sync.container.appendChild(sync.fragment);
    }

    function setSvgAttrs(el, attrs = {}) {
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs)) {
        if (v === null || v === undefined || v === false) {
          el.removeAttribute(k);
        } else {
          el.setAttribute(k, String(v));
        }
      }
      return el;
    }

    function svgEl(name, attrs = {}) {
      const el = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", name);
      return setSvgAttrs(el, attrs);
    }

    function setEdgeHover(id, on) {
      hoveredEdgeId = on ? id : hoveredEdgeId === id ? null : hoveredEdgeId;
      wiresEl.querySelectorAll(`[data-edge-id="${id}"]`).forEach(el => el.classList.toggle("hovered", on));
    }

    function loadEditorState() {
      try {
        const text = localStorage.getItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!text) return null;
        const data = JSON.parse(text);
        return data && typeof data === "object" && data.model ? data : null;
      } catch (_) { return null; }
    }

    function applyPersistedEditorState(data) {
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !data.model) return;
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(data.model)) {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY); } catch (_) {}
        return false;
      }
      const nextModel = normalizeModel(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data.model)));
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(nextModel)) {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY); } catch (_) {}
        return false;
      }
      model = nextModel;
      currentLayerId = data.currentLayerId && byId(data.currentLayerId) ? data.currentLayerId : null;
      selected = normalizeSelection(data.selected || null);
      return true;
    }

    function visibleRectHeight(rect) {
      if (!rect || rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) return 0;
      const top = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight || rect.bottom, rect.top));
      const bottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight || rect.bottom, rect.bottom));
      return Math.max(0, bottom - top);
    }

    function mobilePreviewRuntimeBottomInsetPx() {
      if (!isMobileWorkspaceLayout() || mobileWorkspaceView !== "app") return 0;
      const commandInset = visibleRectHeight(mobileCommandBarEl?.getBoundingClientRect());
      const tabsInset = visibleRectHeight(mobileTabsEl?.getBoundingClientRect());
      const visualViewportBottom = window.visualViewport
        ? window.visualViewport.offsetTop + window.visualViewport.height
        : window.innerHeight;
      const browserInset = Math.max(0, (window.innerHeight || 0) - visualViewportBottom);
      return Math.max(0, Math.round(commandInset + tabsInset + browserInset + 16));
    }

    function normalizeSelection(value = selected) {
      if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
      if ("nodes" in value || "edges" in value) return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges, { allCurrentLayer: value.allCurrentLayer === true });

      if (value.type === "edge") return selectionFromParts([], [value.id]);
      if (value.type === "nodes") return selectionFromParts(value.ids, []);
      if (value.type === "items") return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges);
      return null;
    }

    function parseEditorSnapshot(snapshot) {

      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !data.model) throw new Error("Invalid editor history snapshot.");

    function cancelInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer) {
        clearTimeout(pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer);
        pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer = null;
      }
      pendingInspectorTextCommit = null;
    }

    function flushInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (!pendingInspectorTextCommit) return true;
      const pending = pendingInspectorTextCommit;
      cancelInspectorTextCommit();
      flushingInspectorTextCommit = true;
      let saved = false;
      try {
        saved = saveEditorState(pending.historyKey, pending.sync);
      } finally {
        flushingInspectorTextCommit = false;
      }
      if (saved && pending.draw) scheduleDraw();
      return saved;
    }

    function saveEditorState(historyKey = null, sync = true) {
      if (!flushingInspectorTextCommit && pendingInspectorTextCommit) {
        flushInspectorTextCommit();
      }
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model)) {
        restoreRejectedEditorMutation();
        return false;

        persistModel(sync);

      const historyGroup = historyKey ? `${historyActionId}:${historyKey}` : null;
      if (!historyCurrent) {
        historyCurrent = next;
      } else if (historyGroup && historyGroup === lastHistoryGroup && undoStack.length) {
        // Repeated mutations within one explicit user interaction remain one undo step.

        if (undoStack.length > 100) undoStack.shift();
      }
      redoStack = [];
      historyCurrent = next;
      lastHistoryGroup = historyGroup;
      persistModel(sync);
      updateHistoryButtons();
      return true;
    }

    function undoModel() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      if (!undoStack.length) return;
      const current = editorSnapshot();
      const previous = undoStack.pop();
      redoStack.push(current);
      beginHistoryAction();
      restoreHistorySnapshot(previous);
    }

    function redoModel() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      if (!redoStack.length) return;
      const current = editorSnapshot();
      const next = redoStack.pop();
      undoStack.push(current);
      beginHistoryAction();
      restoreHistorySnapshot(next);
    }

    function validateDataObject(value, path) {
      if (value === undefined) return {};
      assertPlainObject(value, path);
      assertNoNullish(value, path);
      return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
    }

    function validateStateDataValue(value, path) {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        value.forEach((item, index) => validateStateDataValue(item, `${path}[${index}]`));
        return;
      }
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return;
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
        if (!/^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/.test(key)) {
          throw new Error(`${path}.${key} must use a local identifier without dots.`);
        }
        validateStateDataValue(child, `${path}.${key}`);
      }
    }

    function validateStateDataObject(value, path) {
      const data = validateDataObject(value, path);
      validateStateDataValue(data, path);
      return normalizeStateDataObject(data);
    }

    function validateDataTypesObject(value, path, data = {}) {
      if (value === undefined) return {};
      assertPlainObject(value, path);
      const dataObject = normalizeDataObject(data);
      const out = {};
      for (const [key, typeValue] of Object.entries(value)) {
        const cleanKey = validateDataTypePath(key, `${path}.${key}`, { allowEmpty: false });
        if (!dataObjectHasPath(dataObject, cleanKey)) {
          throw new Error(`${path}.${key} must reference a local path declared in state.data.`);
        }
        const type = assertString(typeValue, `${path}.${key}`);
        out[cleanKey] = normalizeStateVariableType(type);
      }
      return out;
    }

    const TRIGGER_MATCH_TYPES = new Set(["realtime"]);
    const TRIGGER_MATCH_OPERATORS = new Set(["equals", "gt", "gte", "lt", "lte", "between"]);

    function normalizeTransitionTriggerMatch(value) {
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return {};
      const field = normalizeBindingPath(value.field || "", "");
      const operator = String(value.operator || "").trim();
      if (!field || !TRIGGER_MATCH_OPERATORS.has(operator)) return {};
      const out = { field, operator };
      if (operator === "between") {
        const range = normalizeTriggerMatchRange(value.value);
        if (!range) return {};
        out.value = range;
      } else {
        out.value = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, "value") ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value.value)) : "";
      }
      return out;
    }

    function transitionTriggerMatchIsEmpty(match) {
      return !isPlainObject(match) || !normalizeBindingPath(match.field || "", "") || !TRIGGER_MATCH_OPERATORS.has(String(match.operator || ""));
    }

    function canonicalTriggerMatchValue(value) {
      if (typeof value === "number") return Number.isFinite(value) ? JSON.stringify(value) : "";
      if (typeof value === "boolean" || typeof value === "string") return JSON.stringify(value);
      if (isPlainObject(value) && Number.isFinite(value.min) && Number.isFinite(value.max)) {
        return JSON.stringify({
          min: value.min,
          minInclusive: value.minInclusive !== false,
          max: value.max,
          maxInclusive: value.maxInclusive !== false
        });
      }
      return "";
    }

    function canonicalTriggerMatchKey(match) {
      const normalized = normalizeTransitionTriggerMatch(match);
      if (transitionTriggerMatchIsEmpty(normalized)) return "*";
      const value = canonicalTriggerMatchValue(normalized.value);
      if (!value) return "";
      return `${normalized.field}:${normalized.operator}:${value}`;
    }

    function triggerMatchOperatorsForSchema(schema) {
      const type = String(schema?.type || "").trim();
      if (type === "number") return new Set(["equals", "gt", "gte", "lt", "lte", "between"]);
      if (["boolean", "text", "email", "url", "image"].includes(type)) return new Set(["equals"]);
      return new Set();
    }

    function normalizeTriggerMatchRange(value) {
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return null;
      const min = Number(value.min);
      const max = Number(value.max);
      if (!Number.isFinite(min) || !Number.isFinite(max)) return null;
      const minInclusive = value.minInclusive

    function jsLiteral(value) {
      return JSON.stringify(value)
        .replace(/</g, "\\u003c")
        .replace(/>/g, "\\u003e")
        .replace(/&/g, "\\u0026")
        .replace(/\u2028/g, "\\u2028")
        .replace(/\u2029/g, "\\u2029");
    }

    function exportImageCanInline(value, path, force = false) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text || /^data:image\//i.test(text)) return false;
      if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(text)) return false;
      return Boolean(force || looksLikeImagePath(path) || looksLikeImageValue(text));
    }

    async function inlineExportImageUrl(value, path, force = false) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!exportImageCanInline(text, path, force)) return value;
      try {
        const response = await fetch(IMAGE_INLINE_URL, {
          method: "POST",
          cache: "no-store",
          credentials: "omit",
          headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ url: text })
        });
        if (!response.ok) return value;
        const payload = await response.json();
        const dataUri = String(payload?.dataUri || "");
        return /^data:image\/[a-z0-9.+-]+;base64,/i.test(dataUri) ? dataUri : value;
      } catch (_) {
        return value;
      }
    }

    async function inlineExportImageValue(value, path = "") {
      if (typeof value === "string") return inlineExportImageUrl(value, path);
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        const out = [];
        for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
          out.push(await inlineExportImageValue(value[index], path));
        }
        return out;
      }
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return value;
      const out = {};
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
        out[key] = await inlineExportImageValue(child, path ? `${path}.${key}` : key);
      }
      return out;
    }

    async function inlineExportImages(payload) {
      const next = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(payload));
      for (const state of next.model.states || []) {
        const statePath = `states.${state.id}`;
        state.data = await inlineExportImageValue(state.data, statePath);
        for (const component of state.components || []) {
          component.url = await inlineExportImageUrl(
            component.url,
            `${statePath}.components.${component.id || "component"}.url`,
            component.type === "image"
          );
        }
      }
      for (const transition of next.model.transitions || []) {
        transition.set = await inlineExportImageValue(transition.set, "transition.set");
      }
      return next;
    }

    function downloadTextFile(text, filename, type) {
      const blob = new Blob([text], { type });
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      a.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      a.remove();
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
    }

    function stateExportSnapshot(s) {
      const snapshot = {
        id: String(s.id),
        title: String(s.title || s.id),
        components: normalizeComponents(s.components || []),
        data: normalizeStateDataObject(s.data),
        dataTypes: normalizeDataTypes(s.dataTypes, s.data),
        dataSource: normalizeDataSource(s.dataSource),
        repeat: normalizeRepeatConfig(s.repeat),
        dataWires: normalizeDataWires(s.dataWires),
        subscriptions: normalizeSubscriptions(s.subscriptions),
        boundary: normalizeBoundaryConfig(s.boundary),
        x: Number(s.x || 0),
        y: Number(s.y || 0)
      };
      if (s.parentId) snapshot.parentId = String(s.parentId);
      return snapshot;
    }

    function isBoundaryFlowTransition(t) {
      return Boolean(t?.boundaryFlow) || isBoundaryProxyId(t?.from) || isBoundaryProxyId(t?.to);
    }

    function componentExportPayload(type, payload) {

    }

    function normalizeDataObject(value) {
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return {};
      return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
    }

    function normalizeStateDataValue(value) {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(normalizeStateDataValue);
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return value;
      const out = {};
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
        out[key] = normalizeStateDataValue(child);
      }
      return out;
    }

    function normalizeStateDataObject(value) {
      return isPlainObject(value) ? normalizeStateDataValue(value) : {};
    }

    function normalizeStateVariableType(value, fallback = "text") {
      const type = String(value || "").trim();
      const allowed = new Set(stateVariableTypes().map(item => item.value));
      if (!allowed.size) return "";
      return allowed.has(type) ? type : (allowed.has(fallback) ? fallback : "");
    }

    function normalizeDataTypePath(path, fallback = "") {
      return normalizeBindingPath(path, fallback);
    }

    function normalizeDataTypes(value, data = {}) {
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return {};
      const dataObject = normalizeDataObject(data);
      const out = {};
      for (const [path, type] of Object.entries(value)) {
        const cleanPath = normalizeDataTypePath(path, "");
        if (cleanPath && dataObjectHasPath(dataObject, cleanPath)) {
          out[cleanPath] = normalizeStateVariableType(type);
        }
      }
      return out;
    }

    function normalizeRealtimeDetailSchema(value) {
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return {};
      const out = {};
      for (const [path, type] of Object.entries(value).slice(0, 64)) {
        const cleanPath = normalizeBindingPath(path, "");
        if (!cleanPath) continue;
        out[cleanPath] = normalizeStateVariableType(type, "text");
      }
      return out;
    }

    function eventFromPoint(point, evt) {
      return {
        clientX: point.clientX,
        clientY: point.clientY,
        button: 0,
        pointerId: point.pointerId ?? evt?.pointerId,
        pointerType: evt?.pointerType || "touch",
        shiftKey: Boolean(evt?.shiftKey),
        preventDefault() { evt?.preventDefault?.(); },
        stopPropagation() { evt?.stopPropagation?.(); }
      };
    }

    function copySelectedNodes() {
      const payload = clipboardSelectionPayload();

    function nodeWidth(s) {
      if (s?.virtualProxy) return GRID_SIZE * 3;
      return NODE_W;
    }

    const PORT_ANCHOR_Y = 24;
    const PORT_SLOT_GAP = 24;
    const PORT_BOTTOM_PAD = 24;

    function nodeHeight(s) {
      if (s?.virtualProxy) return GRID_SIZE * 2;
      try {
        const portCount = Math.max(
          orderedPortTransitions(s.id, "in").length,
          orderedPortTransitions(s.id, "out").length
        );
        return Math.max(NODE_H, PORT_ANCHOR_Y + PORT_SLOT_GAP * Math.max(1, portCount) + PORT_BOTTOM_PAD);
      } catch (_) {
        return NODE_H;
      }
    }

    function nodeAtWorldPoint(p) {
      const states = visibleStates();
      for (let i = states.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const s = states[i];
        if (s.virtualProxy) continue;
        if (p.x >= s.x && p.x <= s.x + nodeWidth(s) && p.y >= s.y && p.y <= s.y + nodeHeight(s)) return s;
      }
      return null;
    }

    function inferStateVariableType(path, value) {
      const key = String(path || "").toLowerCase();
      if (typeof value === "boolean") return "boolean";
      if (typeof value === "number") return "number";
      if (Array.isArray(value)) return "list";
      if (isPlainObject(value)) return "object";
      const text = String(value ?? "");
      if (/(^|[._-])(password|passwort|secret|token|pin)([._-]|$)/i.test(key)) return "password";
      if (/(^|[._-])(email|e-mail|mail)([._-]|$)/i.test(key) || /^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(text)) return "email";
      if (/(^|[._-])(image|img|photo|avatar|logo|icon|picture)([._-]|$)/i.test(key) || /\.(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|svg)(\?|#|$)/i.test(text)) return "image";
      if (/(^|[._-])(url|href|link|website)([._-]|$)/i.test(key) || /^https?:\/\//i.test(text)) return "url";
      return "text";
    }

    function stateVariableTypeRecommendations(path, value) {
      const key = String(path || "").toLowerCase();
      const out = [];
      const add = type => {
        const clean = normalizeStateVariableType(type, "");
        if (clean && !out.includes(clean)) out.push(clean);
      };
      if (Array.isArray(value)) add("list");
      else if (isPlainObject(value)) add("object");
      else if (typeof value === "boolean") add("boolean");
      else if (typeof value === "number") add("number");
      if (/(^|[._-])(password|passwort|secret|token|pin)([._-]|$)/i.test(key)) add("password");
      if (/(^|[._-])(email|e-mail|mail)([._-]|$)/i.test(key)) add("email");
      if (/(^|[._-])(image|images|img|photo|photos|avatar|logo|icon|picture|cover|poster|media|src)([._-]|$)/i.test(key)) {
        add("image");
        add("url");
      }
      if (/(^|[._-])(url|href|link|website|actionurl)([._-]|$)/i.test(key)) add("url");
      if (/(^|[._-])(items|options|rows|columns|keys|avatars|images|submenu|menuitems|tabs|steps)([._-]|$)/i.test(key)) add("list");
      if (/(^|[._-])(open|visible|checked|confirmed|running|finished|active|ring|clicked|enabled)([._-]|$)/i.test(key)) add("boolean");
      if (/(^|[._-])(count|index|duration|max|min|rating|progress|percent|percentage|startedat|endsat|clickedat|subtotal|cartcount)([._-]|$)/i.test(key)) add("number");
      add(inferStateVariableType(path, value));
      add("text");
      return out;
    }

    function repeatValueItems(value) {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;
      if (isColumnarRepeatObject(value)) return columnarRepeatItems(value);
      return null;
    }

    function repeatValueSample(value) {
      const items = repeatValueItems(value);
      if (items) return items.find(item => item && typeof item === "object") ?? items[0] ?? null;
      return value;
    }

    function looksLikeImagePath(path) {
      return /\b(image|images|img|thumbnail|thumb|photo|picture|avatar|cover|poster|logo|icon|media|src)\b/i.test(String(path || ""));
    }

    function looksLikeImageValue(value) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text) return false;
      if (/^data:image\//i.test(text)) return true;
      if (/\.(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif|svg)(?:[?#].*)?$/i.test(text)) return true;

    }

    function imagePathSpecificityScore(path) {
      const parts = String(path || "").split(".").filter(Boolean);
      const first = parts[0] || "";
      const last = parts[parts.length - 1] || "";
      const parents = parts.slice(0, -1).join(".");
      let score = 0;
      if (parts.length <= 2) score += 80;
      else score -= Math.min(90, (parts.length - 2) * 26);
      if (/^(image|images|thumbnail|thumb|photo|photos|picture|pictures|poster|cover|avatar|media)$/i.test(first)) score += 70;
      if (/^(images|photos|pictures|media)$/i.test(first) && /^\d+$/.test(last)) score += 35;
      if (/\b(category|categories|brand|manufacturer|vendor|seller|author|owner|user|profile|meta|metadata|source)\b/i.test(parents)) score -= 75;
      if (/^(image|thumbnail|thumb|photo|picture|poster|cover|avatar)$/i.test(last)) score += 18;
      return score;
    }

    function dataFieldScore(entry, kind) {
      const path = String(entry?.path || "");
      const name = path.split(".").pop() || path;
      const value = entry?.value;
      const text = String(value ?? "");

        if (looksLikeImagePath(path)) score += 70 + imagePathSpecificityScore(path);

        if (/\b(slug|id|uuid|url|image|description|desc|body|content)\b/i.test(name)) score -= 45;
